COR-017 — Integrations

Integración normativa · Guía Maestra v2.17 RC1

Global Integrations es el control plane técnico Organization-scoped para actores externos no humanos. Gobierna
identidad técnica, environments aislados, credentials, technical scopes, rate limits, endpoints externos, webhooks,
technical health y activity contextual; no concede por sí mismo Project access, Effective Entitlement ni UsageRights.

Integration → IntegrationEnvironment → IntegrationCredential / TechnicalScopeGrant / RateLimitPolicy /

WebhookSubscription

```
Dimensión Contrato v2.17
```
```
Ownership Integration pertenece a una Organization; las vistas y mutations se resuelven mediante la Acting Organization actual.
```
```
Project binding Integrations owns ProjectIntegrationBinding; Project Settings es su Project-scoped control surface.
```
```
Environment ProjectIntegrationBinding referencia IntegrationEnvironment exacto. Sandbox y Production están aislados.
```
```
Health Technical Health es derivado: Healthy / Degraded / Unhealthy / Unknown. Unknown cuando falta telemetría.
```
```
Credentials Secrets sólo se muestran en issuance/rotation; después son no recuperables.
```
```
Scopes CredentialScopes ⊆ EnvironmentScopes. Technical scopes no sustituyen Project access ni UsageRights.
```
```
Webhooks Subscription Status y Delivery Health son dimensiones independientes.
```
```
Operations Integrations configura; Operations ejecuta acciones persistentes/long-running cuando proceda.
```
Decisiones de versión

- D134 — Global Integrations is the Organization-scoped technical control plane for non-human external actors.
- D135 — An Integration belongs to one Organization; authorization is evaluated through the current Acting Organization.
- D136 — Integrations owns ProjectIntegrationBinding; Project Settings is the authoritative Project-scoped mutation
surface.
- D137 — ProjectIntegrationBinding binds Project × Integration × IntegrationEnvironment; binding never implies Effective
Access.
- D138 — Sandbox and Production are isolated; credentials and secrets are not shared between environments.
- D139 — CredentialScopes are a subset of EnvironmentScopes and never replace Project access, Effective Entitlement or
UsageRights.
- D140 — Technical Health, lifecycle, binding, delivery health and Effective Access remain independent dimensions.
- D141 — No telemetry means Technical Health Unknown; the UI never fabricates Healthy.
- D142 — Credential secrets and webhook signing secrets are non-retrievable after issuance/rotation.
- D143 — Credential Expiring is a derived temporal condition; canonical lifecycle is Active / Expired / Revoked /
Superseded.
- D144 — WebhookSubscription lifecycle and Webhook Delivery Health are independent.
- D145 — WebhookDelivery represents the logical Event × Subscription delivery; retry attempts are counted/logged
beneath that delivery.
- D146 — Webhook filters may narrow delivery but can never expand authorization.
- D147 — Technical rate limits belong to Integrations; contractual volume limits remain owned by Licensing.
- D148 — Integrations Activity is contextual operational projection; Audit remains durable authoritative evidence.
- D149 — Long-running technical actions may create/link to Operations; Integrations does not create a second execution
engine.
- D150 — SET-04/SET-05 remain Project-scoped control surfaces and are not reopened by Global Integrations.

```
IwantIt v2.18 · Uso interno · Pág. 216IwantIt v2.19 - Uso interno - Pag. 216IwantIt v2.20 - Uso interno - Pag. 222IwantIt v2.21 - Uso interno - Pag. 227
```

```
IwantIt v2.17 · Uso interno · Pág. 217
```
32A. Domain boundary

Global Integrations owns the technical actor and its environment-specific technical configuration. Project Settings controls
Project-scoped binding mutations through Commands into Integrations. Licensing owns commercial authority; Operations
owns persistent execution; Audit owns durable evidence.

- Integration is a non-human actor and never authenticates using a User's personal credentials.
- Technical connectivity ≠ Technical Health ≠ Binding ≠ Effective Access ≠ commercial authority.
- Global Integrations does not own module-specific business configuration.

32B. Canonical entities

Integration is the Organization-owned external technical actor. IntegrationEnvironment is an isolated configuration context.
IntegrationCredential is environment-scoped. TechnicalScopeGrant defines technical capability. ProjectIntegrationBinding
binds a Project to a specific IntegrationEnvironment. WebhookSubscription configures event delivery. RateLimitPolicy
governs technical consumption.

- WebhookDelivery is a logical delivery of one IntegrationEvent to one Subscription and may contain multiple attempts.
- Environment-specific credentials, scopes, endpoints and health are never implicitly inherited across Sandbox/Production.

32C. State dimensions

Integration lifecycle remains Draft → Active ↔ Suspended → Archived. Technical Health is a separate derived read model.
Credential lifecycle is Active / Expired / Revoked / Superseded, with Expiring soon derived from expires_at.
WebhookSubscription is Enabled / Paused / Disabled; Delivery Health is Healthy / Degraded / Failed / Unknown.

- No universal Integration status may collapse independent dimensions.
- Connection tests prove technical connectivity only; they do not prove Project or commercial authorization.

Canonical workflows

WF-INTG-01 · Create Integration

Identity/type → first Environment → scopes/config → optional credential issuance → create. No Project is bound
automatically.

WF-INTG-02 · Configure Environment

Environment → API/config → technical scopes → credentials → technical rate limits → optional webhooks → technical
test.

WF-INTG-03 · Bind to Project

Navigate from Global Integration or Project Settings → SET-04 Project-scoped control → create/update
ProjectIntegrationBinding owned by Integrations → audit.

WF-INTG-04 · Issue Credential

Choose scope subset → expiry/policy → authority/preflight → issue → secret shown once → acknowledge → only
identifier/fingerprint/metadata retained for display.

WF-INTG-05 · Rotate Credential

Impact preflight → replacement credential → optional overlap → validate → predecessor Superseded → revoke/expire;
durable execution may become Operation.

WF-INTG-06 · Revoke Credential

Impact → authority → reason/confirmation → revoke → audit. Revocation never revokes the Integration itself.

WF-INTG-07 · Configure Webhook

Endpoint → events from canonical IntegrationEvent catalogue → restrictive filters → schema/signing → retries → test →
enable → monitor.

WF-INTG-08 · Diagnose

```

```

```
IwantIt v2.17 · Uso interno · Pág. 218
```
Classify issue as connectivity/config/access/licensing/downstream owner → route to correct surface. Do not report every
failure as connectivity failure.

```
IwantIt v2.18 · Uso interno · Pág. 218IwantIt v2.19 - Uso interno - Pag. 218IwantIt v2.20 - Uso interno - Pag. 224IwantIt v2.21 - Uso interno - Pag. 229
```

```
IwantIt v2.17 · Uso interno · Pág. 219
```
Authorization, security y cross-module contracts

Viewing an Integration does not imply authority to issue, rotate or revoke credentials. Sensitive mutations require capability,
impact/preflight, reason when applicable, confirmation and AuditEvent. Minimum capability families:
integration.view/manage, environment.manage, credential.issue/rotate/revoke, scope.manage, webhook.manage,
rate_limit.manage, integration.archive.

INV-INTG-SEC — Secrets are shown only once. After issuance or rotation, UI exposes only
identifier/fingerprint/status/metadata; no copy or reveal action may imply secret retrieval.

Project Settings

SET-04 is the Project-scoped binding control surface. Global Integrations projects the resulting bindings and deep-links
back with exact Project context.

Interactive

SET-05 owns Project/ContentVersion/License File/runtime configuration. Global Integrations owns actor, environment and
technical credentials.

Advertising

Ad Server technical identity/connectivity belongs to Integrations; Campaign/Creative/AdBreak business mappings remain
in Advertising.

Content / Catalog

Provider connectivity belongs to Integrations; imported/reconciled metadata remains owned by Content/Catalog.

Licensing

Technical scopes and technical rate limits never substitute Effective Entitlement, UsageRights or contractual volume terms.

Operations

Persistent/long-running technical actions use Operations; Integrations remains configuration/control surface.

Audit

Activity is contextual; Audit is durable evidence.

Telemetry minimum

- Credential last-used / last authentication.
- API request outcome and response latency.
- Technical rate-limit consumption.
- Webhook delivery outcome, retry count and final response/failure reason.
- Environment health check.
- Staleness must degrade confidence; absent or stale telemetry results in Unknown, never fabricated Healthy.

```

Backlog, Definition of Done y QA — Integrations

P0

- Integration registry
- Environment isolation
- credentials
- technical scopes
- Project-binding projection
- basic Technical Health
- secure rotation/revocation

P1

- technical rate limits
- webhooks
- delivery logs
- Attention
- connection tests

P2

- advanced health analytics
- richer telemetry
- IntegrationEvent Catalogue tooling
- sophisticated replay/recovery
- advanced policy administration

Definition of Done

Integrations is not complete merely because an external API can be called. It must demonstrate environment isolation,
secure non-retrievable secrets, correct Project binding ownership/control, capability-gated mutations, independent
lifecycle/health/access dimensions, authorization-safe Project projections, governed rotation/revocation, webhook delivery
observability, owner-routed Operations, contextual Activity vs durable Audit and explicit separation of technical versus
commercial authority.

- TC-INTG-004 — Sandbox credentials cannot authenticate Production.
- TC-INTG-005 — CredentialScopes cannot exceed EnvironmentScopes.
- TC-INTG-006 — Technical scope never grants Project access or UsageRights.
- TC-INTG-007 — Missing telemetry renders Technical Health Unknown.
- TC-INTG-008 — Active Integration may independently be Unhealthy.
- TC-INTG-009 — Expiring soon derives from expires_at and is not a canonical lifecycle state.
- TC-INTG-010 — Secret shown once at issuance/rotation is not retrievable later.
- TC-INTG-011 — Rotation can create overlap and predecessor Superseded without mutating history.
- TC-INTG-012 — Revoking Credential does not revoke Integration.

Integración normativa · Guía Maestra v2.18 RC1

```
Administration es el Internal Platform Control Plane de IwantIt. Gobierna intención y autoridad administrativa sin duplicar
los owner workspaces globales de Catalog, Operations o Integrations.

Security & Support → owner surfaces

Principio Contrato v2.18

```
Authority No existe shortcut canónico is_admin=true. Toda acción se resuelve server-side por actor, capability, scope, contexto, autoridad y estado.
```
```
Customer access Metadata administrativa no implica acceso a Project/data protegido. Ese acceso requiere autoridad ordinaria o SupportAccessGrant apropiado.

```

```
```
State model Administrative condition, lifecycle, effective status, operational health y Attention permanecen separados.

Decisiones de versión

- D151 — Administration is IwantIt's Internal Platform Control Plane.
- D152 — Internal access is capability-, scope- and authority-gated; there is no implicit super-admin access.
- D153 — Administration does not duplicate Catalog, Operations or Integrations.
- D154 — Organization is the principal administrative navigation context.
- D155 — Licensing has a dedicated Administration workspace.
- D156 — License lifecycle and Effective Entitlement are separate dimensions.
- D157 — Contractual License changes are Commands, not unrestricted CRUD.
- D158 — Renewal adds or supersedes Terms without destroying contractual history.
- D159 — SupportAccessGrant is explicit, scoped, temporary and auditable.
- D160 — Ordinary support does not imply customer Project access.
- D161 — Emergency access uses a dedicated break-glass procedure.
- D162 — Analysis Governance owns provider/model policy, not operational execution.
- D163 — Operations remains the owner of persistent process execution, retries and failures.
- D164 — Audit is transversal; Administration surfaces filtered projections of the same Audit system.
- D165 — Administrative condition, lifecycle, effective status, operational health and attention are separate dimensions.
- D166 — Sensitive administrative Commands require server-side authority checks and Audit.
- D167 — Administration links to owner workspaces instead of creating duplicate management surfaces.
- D168 — Customer Project/data access requires ordinary Project authority or an appropriate SupportAccessGrant.

33A. Authority model

No is_admin=true shortcut is canonical. Administrative authorization is evaluated server-side from Actor + Capability +
Target scope + Organization/Project context + License where applicable + SupportAccessGrant where applicable +
command-specific authority + current domain state.

- Administrative metadata may be visible to appropriately authorized internal roles without a SupportAccessGrant.
- Opening protected customer Project/data for diagnosis requires ordinary Project authority or a valid SupportAccessGrant.

33B. Sensitive Command pattern

Canonical flow: Intent → Command → Preflight → Authority check → Impact preview → Reason (when required) →
Confirmation → Commit/Operation → AuditEvent.

- Applies to License issue/term/suspend/cancel/archive commands.
- Applies to Grant/Extend/Revoke Support Access and Emergency Access.
- Applies to supported Organization restrictions and Analysis Governance mutations.

33C. State and attention rules

License persisted lifecycle is draft · issued · suspended · cancelled · archived. Effective status is derived separately.
Organization has an administrative condition independent from Project, License, Integration and Membership states; the
complete enum is not canonized in COR-018.

- SupportAccessGrant proposal: pending · active · expired · revoked · denied; Expiring soon is Attention on an active grant,
not lifecycle.
- AI model governance separates governance status, rollout and operational health.
- Attention is a derived prioritization read model, never a lifecycle.

Administration surfaces

ADM-01 · Administration Overview

Landing del control plane interno. Jerarquía: Needs Attention → Administration Areas → Recent Administrative Activity +
Other Global Control Planes. Administration Areas: Organizations, Licensing, Analysis Governance, Security & Support.
Catalog, Operations e Integrations son deep-links, no workspaces duplicados.

ADM-02 · Organizations Workspace

One row = one Organization. Search/filter por organization type, administrative condition, products, support access y
attention. Sin bulk selection MVP.

ADM-03 · Organization Full View

Administrative projection de una Organization: Overview, Memberships, Licenses, Projects, Related systems, Activity y
Audit. Internal account owner es metadata opcional; no se crea Contract status agregado ni global Organization renewal
date.

ADM-04 · Licensing Workspace

One row = one License. Lifecycle y effective Status permanecen separados. Current term, Terms & Entitlements,
entitlement explanation, history y Audit. Contractual fields no son unrestricted inline edits.

ADM-05 · Analysis Governance

Provider-neutral governance de providers, models, versions, capabilities, configuration, thresholds, evaluations, rollout y
cost policy. Execution/retries/failures permanecen en Operations.

ADM-06 · Security & Support

Owner surface para SupportAccessGrant, security incidents y Emergency Access. No generic Support pseudo-capability ni
mass approval shortcut.

Semantic corrections aplicadas como contrato de v2.18

Los wireframes se preservan visualmente tal como fueron entregados. Cuando un sample visual entra en
conflicto con las correcciones semánticas explícitas de COR-018, prevalece el contrato escrito siguiente.

Term vs expiration

Si Current effective term es 01 Jan 2026–31 Dec 2026, un valor 17 Apr 2026 no se interpreta como expiración del mismo
term. Debe entenderse, cuando corresponda, como Next rights expiration y asociarse al derecho/Term concreto.

Archive License

Para una License issued + active, las acciones permitidas del sample son Add/Renew Term, Suspend y Cancel. Archive
no está disponible salvo que lifecycle y authority lo permitan.

Support Access Expiring

Expiring / Expiring soon es Attention sobre state=active; no es lifecycle de SupportAccessGrant.

Cross-plane boundaries

Catalog

Administration deep-links; no duplica global identity management.

Operations

Persistent process execution, retries and failures remain owned by Operations.

Integrations

Technical actor/environment/credential control remains in Global Integrations.

Projects

Administrative visibility does not imply protected customer Project/data access.

Audit

Administration consumes filtered projections of the transversal Audit system.

MVP, Definition of Done y QA — Administration

MVP

- ADM-01 Overview
- ADM-02 Organizations
- ADM-03 Organization Full View
- ADM-04 Licensing
- ADM-06 Support Access basic
- Audit projections and cross-links to owner surfaces

MVP-light / next iteration

- ADM-05 Analysis Governance depth
- advanced security incident management
- full Emergency Access workflow
- advanced evaluation datasets
- cost policies
- advanced rollout controls

Definition of Done

Administration no se considera completa por disponer de pantallas internas. Debe demostrar authority server-side sin
super-admin implícito, separación entre metadata administrativa y customer data, Commands sensibles con
preflight/impact/reason/confirmation/Audit, License lifecycle separado de Effective Entitlement, Support Access explícito y
temporal, break-glass dedicado, Analysis Governance separado de Operations, Audit transversal y deep-links hacia owner
surfaces en lugar de duplicarlos.

QA mínimo COR-018

- TC-ADM-001 — No is_admin=true bypass grants universal administrative authority.
- TC-ADM-002 — Administrative metadata view does not grant protected Project/data access.
- TC-ADM-003 — Protected customer access requires ordinary Project authority or valid SupportAccessGrant.
- TC-ADM-004 — Sensitive Commands execute server-side authority check and create AuditEvent.
- TC-ADM-005 — License lifecycle and Effective Status render independently.
- TC-ADM-006 — Renewal adds/supersedes Terms without destroying history.
- TC-ADM-007 — Issued+active License does not expose Archive as an authoritative command.
- TC-ADM-008 — Next rights expiration, when earlier than current term end, identifies the affected right/Term.
- TC-ADM-009 — Organization administrative condition remains independent from Project/License/Integration/Membership
state.
- TC-ADM-010 — Attention never becomes Organization lifecycle.
- TC-ADM-011 — SupportAccessGrant is scoped, temporary and auditable.
- TC-ADM-012 — Expiring soon remains Attention on an active grant.
- TC-ADM-013 — Ordinary support and Emergency Access are distinct workflows.
- TC-ADM-014 — Break-glass captures target, capabilities, reason, incident reference, duration, impact, authority and
Audit.
- TC-ADM-015 — ADM-05 governance changes do not take ownership of execution/retries/failures.
- TC-ADM-016 — Model governance status, rollout and operational health remain separate.
- TC-ADM-017 — Administration Activity does not replace Audit evidence.

COR-019 - Users / IAM

Organization-level Identity & Access Control Plane - Integracion normativa v2.19

Users / IAM gobierna identidad humana, pertenencia organizacional, autoridad funcional, asignacion a Projects y
discoverability de la plataforma. Soporta estudios y organizaciones colaboradoras -incluidas agencias de Product Placement
/ Interactive y Clearance, y futuras agencias de Advertising- que trabajan sobre Projects de multiples clientes desde una
unica Acting Organization.

Identity != Membership != Project Access != Capability != UsageRight != Support Access

```
Principio Contrato v2.19
```
```
Need to know Solo se descubren superficies, entidades y datos necesarios y autorizados.
```
```
Need to access Una surface visible no concede acceso general a recursos o acciones.
```
```
Least privilege La authority efectiva es la interseccion de ceilings, grants, capabilities, estado, visibility y policies.
```
```
Deny by default Lo no concedido explicitamente se considera no autorizado.
```
```
Navigation-aware Global nav, Project nav, tabs, CTAs, search, counts, notifications y activity se componen tras autorizar.
```
```
Backend boundary Toda route, read model y Command se reautoriza server-side.
```
```
No leakage Hidden surfaces no revelan existencia mediante badges, counts, facets, search, activity o errores.
```

```
34.1 Scope y ownership

Users / IAM es el control plane de identidad y acceso a nivel Organization. Es owner conceptual de User,
OrganizationMembership, Invitation, RolePackage, capability assignment y Acting Organization context. Participa en
ProjectAccessGrant sin desplazar SET-02. No posee Licensing/UsageRights, Project lifecycle, SupportAccessGrant,
technical identities ni Audit.

34.2 Modelo multi-Organization y multiestudio

Una Organization colaboradora puede recibir acceso a Projects propiedad de multiples Organizations y asignarlos
internamente. Un editor de Product Placement / Interactive o un profesional de Clearance puede trabajar para varios
estudios desde una unica Acting Organization sin convertirse en Member directo de cada estudio.

34.3 Collaboration ceiling

El ProjectAccessGrant concedido por la owner Organization a la Organization colaboradora define el maximo delegable.
Los assignments individuales pueden concretar o reducir ese techo, nunca ampliarlo. Effective capability se calcula por
interseccion.

34.4 Acting Organization

Un User puede pertenecer a multiples Organizations, pero cada request relevante opera desde una Acting Organization
explicita. Las capabilities/grants de otras Organizations no se suman; el cambio de contexto recompone las proyecciones
autorizadas.

34.5 Entidades canónicas

User - Identidad humana global; account/authentication separado de Membership.

OrganizationMembership - Relacion User x Organization con Role Package, state y validity. No concede Project access
universal.

Invitation - Entidad pre-Membership. PENDING -> ACCEPTED | EXPIRED | REVOKED. No concede authority antes de
aceptarse.

RolePackage - Preset comprensible de capabilities; no es authority final.

Capability - Autoridad funcional granular y backend-first.

ProjectAccessGrant - Source of Truth Project-scoped para Organizations y OrganizationMemberships.

Acting Organization - Contexto explicito de sesion/request.

Effective Access Decision - Read/result model explicable; no sustituye grants/capabilities.

34.6 Lifecycles

Invitation: PENDING -> ACCEPTED | EXPIRED | REVOKED. No DRAFT en MVP.

OrganizationMembership: ACTIVE <-> SUSPENDED; terminal ENDED. INVITED pertenece a Invitation.

User account state: separado. Suspender Membership no desactiva User.

```

```
34.7 Access & Visibility Model

H/R/O/M es una proyeccion UX, no un permiso universal persistido.

```
Nivel UX contract
```
```
H - Hidden No descubrible.
```
```
R - Read Visible y consultable dentro del detalle autorizado.
```
```
O - Operate Operaciones ordinarias autorizadas.
```
```
M - Manage Configuracion, asignacion o Commands sensibles dentro del scope.
```
34.8 Role Package baseline

```
Role Package Superficies minimas / patron
```
```
Organization Owner/Admin Organization IAM + Settings + portfolio controls autorizados.
```
```
Project Manager Summary + coordinacion Content + modulos aplicables + Settings autorizados.
```
```
Content Editor Content + Editor + Analysis de soporte.
```
```
Validator Editor/Core review + Validation + Analysis de soporte.
```
```
Analyst Analysis + source Content/Core read.
```
```
PP / Interactive Editor Content + Editor + Analysis + Interactive.
```
```
Clearance Reviewer Content + Editor contextual + Analysis relevante + Clearance. Advertising Hidden salvo grant adicional.
```
```
Clearance Authority Clearance Reviewer + authority-sensitive Clearance Commands.
```
```
Advertising Manager Content/context read + Analysis projection + Advertising.
```
```
Viewer Solo superficies Read explicitamente concedidas.
```
```
Integration Admin Integrations + Project integration controls autorizados.
```
34.9 Contextual Read Access

Los workflows cross-module reciben solo el acceso minimo necesario. Caso normativo: Clearance Reviewer puede requerir
Content=Read, Editor=Read/contextual, Analysis=relevant projection y Clearance=Operate; Interactive y Advertising
permanecen Hidden salvo authority adicional. Module/License existence no implica discoverability.

```
IwantIt v2.20 - Uso interno - Pag. 245IwantIt v2.21 - Uso interno - Pag. 250
```

```
IwantIt v2.19 - Uso interno - Pag. 240
```
34.10 Workflows canónicos

IAM-WF-01 Invite member - Email -> Role Package -> optional Project assignment -> Invitation PENDING.

IAM-WF-02 Accept invitation - Authenticate/create User -> accept -> Membership ACTIVE -> grants autorizados efectivos.

IAM-WF-03 Resend/Revoke - Opera sobre Invitation sin borrar historico.

IAM-WF-04 Change Role Package - Preflight -> capability impact -> authority -> confirm -> Audit.

IAM-WF-05 Suspend Membership - Pierde authority en esa Organization; preserva User, grants e historico.

IAM-WF-06 End Membership - Impact preflight -> end -> effective authority desaparece -> Audit.

IAM-WF-07 Manage Project assignments - Usa los mismos ProjectAccessGrant contracts que SET-02.

IAM-WF-08 Switch Acting Organization - Cambia contexto server-side y recompone el universo autorizado.

IAM-WF-09 Explain Effective Access - Member x Project x Surface -> H/R/O/M + decision trail.

34.11 Sensitive IAM Commands

Intent -> Preflight -> Authority -> Impact -> Reason cuando aplique -> Confirmation -> Commit ->

AuditEvent.

Ningun Command puede dejar una Organization sin la autoridad administrativa minima requerida.

34.12 Integracion con SET-02 y Administration

SET-02: Who can access this Project? IAM-02: Which Projects can this member access? Son proyecciones reciprocas
sobre la misma Source of Truth y deben enlazarse bidireccionalmente.

Administration sigue siendo el Internal Platform Control Plane. ADM-03 Memberships es proyeccion administrativa interna,
no Organization Team & Access. SupportAccessGrant sigue separado.

```
IwantIt v2.20 - Uso interno - Pag. 246IwantIt v2.21 - Uso interno - Pag. 251
```

```
IwantIt v2.19 - Uso interno - Pag. 241
```
34.13 Decisiones de versión D169-D186

- D169 - Users/IAM is the Organization-level Identity & Access Control Plane.
- D170 - Need-to-know and need-to-access govern discoverability as well as authorization.
- D171 - Navigation is authorization-aware and composed after effective access resolution.
- D172 - OrganizationMembership never implies universal Project access.
- D173 - A collaborating Organization may access Projects owned by multiple Organizations from one Acting Organization.
- D174 - Organization collaboration grants define the maximum delegable authority for client Projects.
- D175 - Individual member assignments may reduce but never expand the collaboration ceiling.
- D176 - Invitation is a distinct pre-Membership entity and grants no authority before acceptance.
- D177 - Role Packages provide capability presets but are not the final authority source.
- D178 - H/R/O/M are UX projections of effective authority, not persisted universal permission levels.
- D179 - Cross-module workflows receive only the minimum contextual access required.
- D180 - Module/License existence does not imply discoverability for every Project actor.
- D181 - IAM and SET-02 are reciprocal projections over the same Project access Source of Truth.
- D182 - Organization IAM and internal Administration are separate control planes.
- D183 - Membership suspension does not disable the global User identity or destroy access history.
- D184 - Privileged IAM Commands require server-side authority, impact preflight and Audit.
- D185 - IAM must prevent administrative lockout of an Organization.
- D186 - Effective Access must be explainable from the contributing authority and restriction layers.

34.14 MVP, Definition of Done y QA

P0 / MVP

- IAM-01, IAM-02 e IAM-03.
- OrganizationMembership, Invitation, standard Role Packages y Project assignment.
- Multi-client Organization + collaboration ceiling + Acting Organization.
- Capability-aware navigation, need-to-know, need-to-access y no-existence-leakage.
- Membership suspend/end, sensitive-command Audit y SET-02 <-> IAM integration.

P1

- IAM-04 Effective Access Inspector completo.
- Advanced access explanation, role/capability comparison, expiring access attention y advanced assignments.

P2

- Custom Role Builder, delegated administration avanzada, access requests/certification, JIT access y field-level policies
adicionales solo donde exista necesidad validada.

Definition of Done

Users/IAM queda completo cuando demuestra separacion User/Membership/ProjectAccessGrant, Acting Organization sin
mezcla de authority, colaboracion multiestudio, ceiling no ampliable, Role Package capability-first, navegacion/datos
need-to-know, reautorizacion backend, contextual access minimo, Invitation sin authority previa, suspension sin destruir
identidad/historico, reciprocidad SET-02, separacion de Administration y proteccion contra administrative lockout.

QA mínimo TC-IAM-001..020

- TC-IAM-001 - User en dos Organizations no combina authority.
- TC-IAM-002 - Switch Acting Organization invalida projections anteriores.
- TC-IAM-003 - Membership sin Project access no descubre Project.
- TC-IAM-004 - Agency accede a Projects de tres owners desde una Acting Organization.
- TC-IAM-005 - Individual assignment inferior al ceiling funciona.
- TC-IAM-006 - Assignment que supera ceiling: denied.
- TC-IAM-007 - Role capability no concedida por client grant: ineffective.
- TC-IAM-008 - Client grant capability ausente en Role: ineffective.
- TC-IAM-009 - Clearance Reviewer sin advertising.view no descubre Advertising.
- TC-IAM-010 - Direct URL a Advertising sin authority: denied.
- TC-IAM-011 - Hidden Advertising no aparece en search/count/facets/activity.
- TC-IAM-012 - Contextual Editor Read no permite unauthorized Edit.
- TC-IAM-013 - Pending Invitation no concede authority.
- TC-IAM-014 - Accepted Invitation crea Membership.
- TC-IAM-015 - Suspended Membership pierde authority y conserva history.
- TC-IAM-016 - Ended Membership no borra Audit/history.
- TC-IAM-017 - Last privileged admin no puede eliminarse sin replacement.
- TC-IAM-018 - IAM mutation se refleja en SET-02.
- TC-IAM-019 - SET-02 mutation se refleja en IAM-02.
- TC-IAM-020 - Support user sin SupportAccessGrant no obtiene customer Project/data access.

```
Purpose Apply the external audit corrections without silently rewriting the
historical v2.21 baseline.

```
Precedence rule
This addendum is normative for v2.22. Where a clause below conflicts with the v2.21 baseline included in the composite Guide, this
addendum prevails. Unaffected v2.21 content remains in force. No conflict may be resolved by implementation convenience.
```
**1. Purpose and freeze position**

```
v2.22 closes the gaps identified by the independent product and architecture freeze audit. It does not change the product
thesis or enlarge M0 into M1/M2. Its purpose is to make the M0 contract deterministic enough that a separate
implementation team can build it without inventing product or security semantics.
 M0 remains Understand the Content: create, ingest, analyse, structure, correct, validate and re-enter the governed
Core.
 M1/M2 remain responsible for product serving, complete licensing administration, advanced integrations, Advertising,
Interactive and Clearance end-to-end.
 Reference UI remains non-authoritative where incidental data/copy conflicts with the written contract.
 Any structural change after freeze requires an ADR and owner approval.
```
**2. New normative decisions D199-D218**

**ID TITLE NORMATIVE DECISION**

D199 Managed authentication provider

```
IwantIt uses a managed identity provider for
authentication; identity provider authenticates,
IwantIt authorizes.
```
D200 WorkOS AuthKit M0

```
WorkOS AuthKit Hosted UI is the M0 provider
behind AuthenticationProviderPort; production use
is subject to DPA/security/residency review. Auth0
EU is the approved fallback if strict EU identity
residency cannot be contractually satisfied.
```
D201 Provider-independent identity

```
IwantIt User/AuthIdentity/ApplicationSession remain
canonical; WorkOS Organization/Role/Permission
are not product authorization truth.
```
D202 Prospect bootstrap

```
Verified self-service prospect onboarding may
bootstrap the first Organization and
ORGANIZATION_ADMIN Membership atomically;
no email-domain autojoin.
```
D203 Support access

```
Internal staff have no standing customer
Project/data access. Time-limited
SupportAccessGrant is mandatory for support
access.
```
D204 New Project reconciliation

```
NPW remains short: title required, Content Type
optional/progressive, video optional at form level;
Analyse content is primary when media exists;
Create project without video is the secondary path.
```

```
IwantIt | Content Intelligence Infrastructure
```
```
Confidential - IwantIt
```
**ID TITLE NORMATIVE DECISION**

D205 Initial Analysis Policy

```
Initial Analysis is governed by a versioned policy for
allowed media, quota/rate, feature/profile, provider,
cost and review policy. Retry does not consume a
new logical scan.
```
D206 Technical asset replacement

```
A new AssetFile may remain in the same
ContentVersion only after editorial and temporal
equivalence assessment; uncertainty routes to a
new ContentVersion/remapping.
```
D207 SceneStructure aggregate

```
SceneStructure is the aggregate root for ordered
Scenes. A governed VALID structure covers the
exact ContentVersion duration without gaps or
invalid overlaps.
```
D208 Optimistic concurrency Sensitive Core/IAM writes use revision + expected_revision; last-write-wins is forbidden.

D209 Unified Taxonomy

```
Taxonomy/TaxonomyVersion/Taxon/
TaxonomyAssignment is the sole taxonomy engine.
Inventory Family and Context are
projections/assignments over it.
```
D210 Inventory split and lineage

```
SplitInventoryItem is a first-class governed
command preserving appearance redistribution,
canonical/taxonomy review, validation impact and
lineage.
```
D211 Appearance completion

```
Appearance has explicit lifecycle, modality,
provenance, exact ContentVersion and revision.
Counts/time/coverage are derived, not editable
facts.
```
D212 Dimensional validation

```
ValidationDecision is immutable and dimension-
specific; current validation state is a projection and
material mutations can cause
NEEDS_REVALIDATION.
```
D213 Analysis reproducibility

```
AnalysisRun freezes exact inputs in
AnalysisInputManifest including source checksums,
SceneStructure, Core/Validation baseline,
taxonomy versions, rules/config and
provider/model.
```
D214 Analysis currentness

```
AnalysisSnapshot currentness is derived from
dependency comparison; OUTDATED remains
historical and is not silently rewritten.
```
D215 Scoped vertical relevance

```
Vertical relevance can be assigned to
InventoryItem, Appearance, Scene or
ContextAssignment; item relevance never silently
propagates to all appearances.
```
D216 M0 entitlement provisioning

```
M0 uses real
License/LicenseTerm/UsageRightGrant entities and
audited internal pilot provisioning; teaser access is
policy, not bypass flags.
```
D217 M0 Screen Contract Matrix

```
Every M0 required/foundation screen has a written
screen contract. Written contract prevails over
incidental wireframe sample data/copy.
```
D218 Operational closure baseline

```
M0 freeze includes WorkContext/deep-link protocol,
manual relationship foundation, retention,
SLO/RPO/RTO, WCAG 2.2 AA, complete
traceability and final external re-audit.
```
**3. FRZ-01 - Authentication and identity bootstrap**

```
Decision
Managed Identity Provider confirmed. WorkOS AuthKit Hosted UI is the M0 authentication provider behind an IwantIt-owned
adapter. Auth0 EU is the approved fallback if production identity-data residency cannot be contractually satisfied by WorkOS.
```

```
IwantIt | Content Intelligence Infrastructure
```
```
Confidential - IwantIt
```
**3.1 Authentication is not authorization**

```
Authentication -> WHO is this human?
Authorization -> WHAT may this authenticated human know, see and do here?
```
The identity provider may establish authentication, verification and provider session primitives. It never owns IwantIt
Organization membership, Project authority, module visibility, licensing rights or Acting Organization semantics.

**3.2 Canonical identity objects**

```
User {
id
display_name
account_state: ACTIVE | SUSPENDED
created_at
updated_at
}
```
```
AuthIdentity {
id
user_id
provider: WORKOS
provider_subject
email_normalized
email_verified
created_at
last_authenticated_at
}
UNIQUE(provider, provider_subject)
```
```
ApplicationSession {
session_id
user_id
acting_organization_id?
authenticated_at
last_seen_at
expires_at
authentication_strength
revoked_at?
}
```
 Email is a contact and identity-matching attribute, never the domain primary key or authorization key.
 Customer-facing bootstrap and invitation acceptance require a verified identity.
 Acting Organization is application-session/request context; User.current_organization_id remains prohibited.
 Global User suspension revokes application sessions without rewriting historical Memberships or Project grants.
 Membership suspension remains Organization-local and must not globally disable the User.

**3.3 WorkOS boundary and anti-lock-in**

```
AuthenticationProviderPort
startAuthentication()
completeAuthentication()
getVerifiedIdentity()
requestVerification()
logoutProviderSession()
revokeProviderSessions()
```
```
M0 adapter: WorkOSAuthKitAdapter
```
No domain package may import WorkOS SDK types. WorkOS Organizations, Roles and Permissions are not mapped into
IwantIt authority. Provider identifiers are contained at AuthIdentity and provider-adapter boundaries.

```
Production caveat
Before production: complete WorkOS DPA, subprocessor, security and identity-data residency review. Public documentation does
not establish a strict EU residency guarantee for AuthKit identity data equivalent to an EU-region tenant. If this cannot be
contractually satisfied when required, use the approved Auth0 EU adapter without changing IwantIt IAM.
```

```
IwantIt | Content Intelligence Infrastructure
```
```
Confidential - IwantIt
```
**3.4 Self-service prospect and first Organization bootstrap**

```
Verified sign-up -> BootstrapOrganization -> Organization + ACTIVE ORGANIZATION_ADMIN v1 Membership
+ Acting Organization + Audit/outbox -> Quick Create
```
```
 BootstrapOrganization is allowed only for a verified User with zero active Memberships in M0 self-service onboarding.
 Organization name is the only required Organization bootstrap input; licensing, modules, team and integrations stay
out.
 No auto-join by email domain.
 Retry is idempotent and cannot create duplicate Organizations or Memberships.
 Users who already have an active Membership do not receive an unrestricted self-service create-tenant feature.
```
**3.5 Invitation identity continuation**

```
 An invitation remains pre-authority until accepted.
 A new invited human may authenticate/register, verify the invited email and return to the same pending invitation.
 An authenticated identity whose verified email does not match the invited identity cannot accept.
 Existing ACTIVE Membership makes the invitation already satisfied; SUSPENDED/ENDED Membership is never
silently reactivated.
 Acceptance is atomic: lock Invitation -> validate state/expiry/identity/package -> create Membership -> mark
ACCEPTED -> Audit/outbox.
 Accepting an invitation never silently switches away from an already-active Acting Organization when multiple
Memberships exist.
```
**3.6 Session security**

```
 Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite application session cookie; CSRF protection where applicable.
 Provider/access/refresh credentials stay server-side and never enter localStorage, URLs, analytics or Audit.
 Validated internal return_to only; reauthorize intended deep link after authentication.
 Account recovery and sign-in responses must not become account-enumeration side channels.
 Rate limits and abuse controls apply to login, registration, verification, recovery, invitations and Organization bootstrap.
```
**4. FRZ-02 - Role packages and Support Access**

```
RolePackageVersion remains a preset of potential capabilities. It is never sufficient Project authority; ProjectAccessGrant,
collaboration ceiling, Data Visibility, policy and entitlement continue to intersect at request time.
```

**4.1 Internal support boundary**

```
SupportAccessGrant {
id
support_membership_id
project_id
capability_scope[]
reason
support_case_reference?
valid_from
valid_until
state: ACTIVE | REVOKED | EXPIRED
issued_by_user_id
revoked_by_user_id?
created_at
revoked_at?
}
```
```
 Default M0 validity: 4 hours; maximum: 24 hours.
 INTERNAL_ADMIN has internal control-plane capabilities only; it is not a standing customer-data superuser.
 Customer Project/data access requires a current SupportAccessGrant and is intersected with Data Visibility/domain
policy.
 SupportAccessGrant never becomes a License, UsageRightGrant or customer ProjectAccessGrant.
 Issue, use, revoke and expiry are auditable; reads may also create security telemetry without logging sensitive
payloads.
```
**5. FRZ-03 - New Project and Initial Analysis Policy**

```
NPW-01: Project title (required) + Content Type (optional/progressive) + Video (optional at form
level)
Primary when media exists: Analyse content
Secondary: Create project without video
```
```
 Create without video atomically creates Project + creator ProjectAccessGrant + Content + CV-001 DRAFT and routes
to Project Overview.
 Analyse content remains a single-intent flow: media upload/claim, Project foundation and automatic Initial Analysis. No
second Start button.
 Team, licensing, modules, territory and integrations do not return to the wizard.
```

```
IwantIt | Content Intelligence Infrastructure
```
```
Confidential - IwantIt
```
```
InitialAnalysisPolicyVersion {
id, version
allowed_media_profiles
max_file_size
max_duration
free_scan_policy
rerun_policy
analysis_feature_profile
provider_profile
queue_priority
rate_policy
cost_guard
human_review_policy
effective_from
}
```
 Policy values are versioned data/configuration, not scattered hardcoded branches.
 Technical Operation/Attempt retry does not consume a new logical free/billable scan.
 Logical initial analysis identity binds exact ContentVersion, source AssetFile/checksum, policy version and analysis
profile version.
 InitialAnalysisPort is the stable application boundary used by Quick Create before/after the concrete Analysis adapter is
implemented.

**6. FRZ-04 - Technical Asset replacement**

```
TechnicalReplacementAssessment.outcome = EQUIVALENT | NOT_EQUIVALENT | UNCERTAIN
```
 Same ContentVersion is permitted only when the replacement represents the same editorial cut and preserves
compatible normalized temporal correspondence.
 Duration equality alone is not proof. Assessment considers technical profile, start/offset, insert/delete risk, sampled
correspondence and downstream truth.
 When governed Scene/Appearance/Evidence/Analysis truth already exists, UNCERTAIN cannot stay in the same
ContentVersion.
 Equivalent replacement creates a new ACTIVE AssetFile and SUPERSEDES the old file; historical Evidence/Analysis
continue to reference their exact original AssetFile/checksum.
 NOT_EQUIVALENT or UNCERTAIN routes to CreateContentVersion/governed remapping; no source overwrite.

**7. FRZ-05 - SceneStructure and optimistic concurrency**

```
SceneStructure {
id
content_version_id
revision
timestamps
}
Scene { id, scene_structure_id, start_ms, end_ms, label?, provenance }
Derived integrity: INCOMPLETE | VALID | INVALID
```
 Authoring may be INCOMPLETE; governed VALID requires first.start=0, last.end=ContentVersion.duration, ordered
Scenes, no gaps and no invalid overlaps.
 CreateScene, AdjustScene, SplitScene, MergeScenes and ValidateSceneStructure are canonical commands.
 Moving a Scene boundary never silently moves an Appearance.
 SceneStructure, InventoryItem, Appearance, CanonicalLink and sensitive ProjectAccessGrant mutations use revision +
expected_revision.
 Revision mismatch returns 409 CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION; last-write-wins is forbidden.

**8. FRZ-06 - Unified Taxonomy**

```
Taxonomy -> TaxonomyVersion -> Taxon -> TaxonomyAssignment
```
 This is the sole taxonomy engine.
 M0 uses INVENTORY and CONTEXT taxonomies; later Advertising/Clearance taxonomies reuse the same
abstraction.
 Inventory Family is a governed Inventory Taxonomy classification/presentation, not a Family table.
 Key Context is a Context TaxonomyAssignment to an exact Project/ContentVersion/Scene/range as applicable, not a
parallel ContextTerm authority.
 Assignments preserve the exact TaxonomyVersion; a new version never reinterprets history silently.


```
IwantIt | Content Intelligence Infrastructure
```
```
Confidential - IwantIt
```
**9. FRZ-07 - Inventory and Appearance completion**

```
InventoryLineage { relation: MERGED_FROM | SPLIT_FROM, source_item_id, target_item_id, created_at }
```
```
Appearance {
id, inventory_item_id, content_version_id
start_ms, end_ms
lifecycle: ACTIVE | SUPERSEDED | ARCHIVED
modalities[]: VISUAL | ON_SCREEN_TEXT | AUDIBLE | MENTIONED
provenance
revision
}
```
 SplitInventoryItem is a first-class governed command. It redistributes explicit Appearance IDs, preserves lineage and
triggers review of CanonicalLinks, taxonomy and validation impact.
 InventoryItem lifecycle remains ACTIVE/MERGED/ARCHIVED; split history lives in lineage rather than a synthetic
SPLIT lifecycle.
 Appearance count, total on-screen time and Scene coverage are derived. Total on-screen time is the union of active
appearance intervals, not a naive sum.
 Appearance remains exact-ContentVersion temporal truth and one InventoryItem remains one Editor track projection.

**10. FRZ-08 - Dimensional Validation**

```
ValidationDimension = IDENTITY | TEMPORAL | SPATIAL | MODALITY | TAXONOMY | RELATIONSHIP
ValidationDecision.decision = VALIDATE | REJECT | CORRECT_AND_VALIDATE
Projection per dimension = NOT_REVIEWED | IN_REVIEW | VALIDATED | REJECTED | NEEDS_REVALIDATION
```
 ValidationDecision is immutable and references subject type, ID, revision, dimension, actor, Membership, Acting
Organization, Evidence, reason, correlation ID and timestamp.
 CORRECT_AND_VALIDATE first executes the owner-domain mutation and then validates the resulting revision;
Validation does not own corrected domain data.
 ValidationImpactPolicy maps a material mutation to affected validation dimensions and can move a previously
VALIDATED dimension to NEEDS_REVALIDATION without deleting history.
 AI may propose/confidence/evidence but cannot silently create human validation authority in M0.

**11. FRZ-09 - Analysis reproducibility and currentness**

```
AnalysisInputManifest (immutable)
```
- ContentVersion
- AssetFile IDs + SHA-256
- MediaTechnicalProfile version
- SceneStructure revision/hash
- Inventory baseline/hash
- Validation baseline/hash
- TaxonomyVersion IDs
- ruleset/configuration versions
- provider/model

```
AnalysisSnapshot currentness = CURRENT | OUTDATED (derived)
```
 AnalysisRun stores Analysis profile/methodology/policy versions, provider/model, immutable input manifest, output
reference, state, timing and cost observations.
 Absent inputs are explicitly EMPTY rather than omitted ambiguously.
 Provider adapters declare capability/input/output/model/cost/region/DataVisibility/health metadata.
 OUTDATED reasons include ContentVersion/source/SceneStructure/Inventory/Validation/Taxonomy/methodology/
profile changes. Old snapshots remain historical.
 VerticalRelevanceAssignment supports InventoryItem, Appearance, Scene and ContextAssignment; item-level
relevance never auto-propagates to every Appearance.
 Analysis Workspace contract includes appearance-oriented review, filters/authorized counters, candidate
materialization, identity consolidation, taxonomy, validation, Person-to-Character, relationships, vertical relevance and
Open in Editor with return context.

**13. FRZ-11 - M0 Screen Contract Matrix**

```
Every REQUIRED or FOUNDATION M0 screen must have a written contract specifying route, purpose, actors, queries,
commands, capabilities, entitlements, exact-version behavior, non-happy states, deep-link/return protocol, owning packets
and acceptance tests. The companion M0 Screen Contract Matrix v1.0 is normative at implementation level.
```
```
Reference UI rule
Approved wireframes remain the visual baseline. Incidental sample copy, counts, names, status values or controls in a raster do not
create domain truth. Written screen contract and architecture rules prevail.
```
**14. FRZ-12 - WorkContext, deep links, foundations and**

**relationships**

```
WorkContext (non-authoritative) {
user_id, acting_organization_id
project_id, content_version_id
surface
scene_id?, inventory_item_id?, appearance_id?, timecode_ms?
view_state?
}
restore -> authenticate -> validate Acting Organization -> EffectiveAccessResolver -> exact
resource authorization
```
```
 Use opaque session-bound return_context_id for Analysis -> Editor -> return workflows; do not encode a large
sensitive state blob in URLs.
 M0 customer Project ownership transfer is deferred. Archive and Restore remain; collaboration handles normal multi-
Organization work.
 Integration foundation names are reserved: Integration -> IntegrationEnvironment -> IntegrationCredential ->
TechnicalScopeGrant -> ProjectIntegrationBinding. Technical scope is never Project access or UsageRight.
 M0 internal Administration is limited to SupportAccessGrant, pilot entitlement provisioning, audit lookup and
account/security control.
 M0 includes minimum manual ContextualRelationship with exact Project/ContentVersion context, optional
Scene/range, provenance and revision; advanced relationship automation is deferred.
```
**15. FRZ-13 - Retention, reliability and accessibility baseline**

**DATA CLASS M0 DEFAULT**

Unclaimed UploadSession object Delete 24 h after expiry

Worker temporary files Immediate cleanup; orphan reconciliation <=24 h

Invalid abandoned upload Delete <=72 h after terminal invalid/abandon

Active source AssetFile No TTL; governed deletion only

Superseded source Preserve while referenced/history requires


```
IwantIt | Content Intelligence Infrastructure
```
```
Confidential - IwantIt
```
**DATA CLASS M0 DEFAULT**

Playback proxy / FRAME_UI Reconstructable; GC eligible after 30 d unreferenced

Evidence artifact Governed; no cache-TTL deletion

AnalysisRun/Snapshot/Candidate Historical until governed Project deletion

ValidationDecision Immutable history

Audit 24 months baseline

Application logs 30 days

Auth/security telemetry 90 days baseline

**TARGET M0 BASELINE**

Customer API/session availability 99.9% monthly

Ordinary API read p95 <500 ms

Ordinary mutation p95 <1 s

Home/Projects backend p95 <1.5 s

Operation dispatch p95 <5 s after commit

Media-access issuance p95 <750 ms

RPO <=15 min

RTO <=4 h

```
 Long-running provider/transcoding time is excluded from ordinary request SLO; committed Operation durability is
mandatory.
 Backup configuration alone is insufficient: restore must be tested.
 M0 accessibility target is WCAG 2.2 AA: keyboard, focus, semantic structure, contrast/non-color status, accessible
forms/tables/player/timecode, associated errors, dialog focus management, reduced motion and announced
progress/status.
```
**16. FRZ-14 - Traceability contract**

```
Guide Decision -> invariant -> entity/command/query -> Development Packet -> screen/API -> test ->
gate
```
```
 Stable identifier families: DEC, INV, ENT, CAP, CMD, QRY, EVT, SCR, PKT, TC, GATE and ADR.
 Reverse traceability is mandatory: every M0 screen has an owner packet; every packet has Guide basis; every
capability has consumers; every command has tests; every gate has executable evidence.
 The companion Correction Register and Traceability Matrix is part of the M0 freeze set.
```
**17. FRZ-15 - Cross-document precedence and reconciliation**

```
Guide v2.22 composite -> Architecture Freeze / registries -> Greenfield v1.1 -> Screen Contracts ->
Reference UI
```
```
 One canonical name and lifecycle per concept across all artifacts.
 Capabilities must match package registry, screen visibility, packet authorization and tests.
 No screen referenced by M0 may lack a contract.
 Nothing can be M0 in one document and M1/M2 in another without an explicit scope reconciliation note.
 Corrections are recorded with old/new/reason/affected artifacts; no silent reconciliation.
```

**19. Freeze criteria**

```
 P0 findings = 0 after document remediation and final independent re-audit.
 All M0 Sources of Truth explicit and non-duplicated.
 All M0 REQUIRED/FOUNDATION screens have contracts.
 All critical packets deterministic enough to avoid implementation invention.
 All cross-document conflicts resolved and recorded.
 All strategic decisions recorded, including authentication provider boundary.
 All M0 gates have executable evidence.
 No undocumented architecture/product decision is delegated to Codex.
```
